Current source of the opcode bodies:\n\n```cpp\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_distort_tanh(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]) : nullptr;\n    const float* wet_level = (inst.inputs[3] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]) : nullptr;\n\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) {\n        kernels::distort_tanh_avx2(input, stereo_in ? input_r : nullptr, drive, dry_level,\n                                   wet_level, out_l, out_r);\n        return;\n    }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n        float d = std::max(0.1f, drive[i]);\n        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_level, i, 0.0f);\n        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_level, i, 1.0f);\n        float x_l = input[i];\n        float x_r = stereo_in ? input_r[i] : x_l;\n        out_l[i] = drywet::mix(x_l, std::tanh(x_l * d), dry, wet);\n        out_r[i] = drywet::mix(x_r, std::tanh(x_r * d), dry, wet);\n    }\n}\n\n[[gnu::always_inline]]\ninline void op_distort_soft(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n    float* out_r = ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.out_buffer + 1));\n    const float* input = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[0]);\n    const bool stereo_in = (inst.flags & InstructionFlag::STEREO_INPUT) != 0;\n    const float* input_r = stereo_in\n        ? ctx.buffers->get(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(inst.inputs[0] + 1))\n        : nullptr;\n    const float* threshold = ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[1]);\n    const float* dry_level = (inst.inputs[2] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[2]) : nullptr;\n    const float* wet_level = (inst.inputs[3] != 0xFFFF) ? ctx.buffers->get(inst.inputs[3]) : nullptr;\n\n    auto soft = [](float x) {\n        if (x > 3.0f) return 1.0f;\n        if (x < -3.0f) return -1.0f;\n        float x2 = x * x;\n        return x * (27.0f + x2) / (27.0f + 9.0f * x2);\n    };\n\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) {\n        kernels::distort_soft_avx2(input, stereo_in ? input_r : nullptr, threshold, dry_level,\n                                   wet_level, out_l, out_r);\n        return;\n    }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) {\n        float t = std::clamp(threshold[i], 0.1f, 2.0f);\n        float dry = drywet::coeff(dry_level, i, 0.0f);\n        float wet = drywet::coeff(wet_level, i, 1.0f);\n        float x_l = input[i];\n        float x_r = stereo_in ? input_r[i] : x_l;\n        out_l[i] = drywet::mix(x_l, soft(x_l / t) * t, dry, wet);\n        out_r[i] = drywet::mix(x_r, soft(x_r / t) * t, dry, wet);\n    }\n}\n```\n\nCost on the gate machine (median ns per opcode-block, lower is better):\n\n| Opcode | Original scalar | Current (accepted so far) | Speedup so far |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `op_distort_tanh` | 974.44 | 89.69 | 10.86x |\n| `op_distort_soft` | 257.33 | 43.47 | 5.92x |\n\n## Accepted so far (already in the tree)\n\n```diff\ndiff --git a/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp b/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\nindex 4a294b4..0c65258 100644\n--- a/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\n+++ b/cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/distortion.hpp\n@@ -6,11 +6,177 @@\n #include \"dsp_state.hpp\"\n #include \"dsp_utils.hpp\"\n #include \"drywet.hpp\"\n+#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n #include <cmath>\n #include <algorithm>\n \n namespace cedar {\n \n+namespace kernels {\n+#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n+\n+// Exp(x) on [-80, 80]: range-reduce to r = x - round(x / ln2) * ln2 and use\n+// a degree-7 Taylor polynomial for exp(r); scale by 2^round(x / ln2). Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. You may only edit: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`, `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   Exception: op_distort_tanh call `tanh`/`tan`; for these only, an approximation is allowed if (a) you mark it with a `// simd: approx` comment naming the replaced function, and (b) its peak error stays at or below -100 dBFS (about 1e-5 absolute) on every stimulus.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel. CPU detection is already done\n   once at start-up; never detect features per block or per sample.\n6. The change must make at least one listed opcode **\u2265 5 % faster** than the\n   *current* version (outside measurement noise) and none slower.\n\n## Budget\n\nAt most **10 runs of the check command** and **30 minutes** for\nthis attempt.     // 1/6\n    43\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(0.5f));\n    44\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n    45\t    p = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(p, r), _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f));\n    46\t\n    47\t    __m256i n = _mm256_cvttps_epi32(fx);\n    48\t    n = _mm256_add_epi32(n, _mm256_set1_epi32(127));\n    49\t    n = _mm256_slli_epi32(n, 23);\n    50\t    return _mm256_mul_ps(p, _mm256_castsi256_ps(n));\n    51\t}\n    52\t\n    53\t// simd: approx tanh \u2014 use exp_taylor_avx2 for tanh(x) = (e^(2x)-1)/(e^(2x)+1),\n    54\t// replacing std::tanh.  Peaks stay well below -100 dBFS on every stimulus.\n    55\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline __m256 tanh_exp_avx2(__m256 x) noexcept {\n    56\t    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0f);\n    57\t    x = _mm256_div_ps(_mm256_sub_ps(e, one), _mm256_add_ps(e, one));\n    61\t}\n    62\t\n    63\t// simd: approx tanh \u2014 tanh_exp_avx2 above, used instead of std::tanh.\n    64\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline _mm256_mul_ps(y, wet));\n    85\t            _mm256_store_ps(out_l + i, out_v);\n    86\t            _mm256_store_ps(out_r + i, out_v);\n    87\t        }\n    88\t        return;\n    89\t    }\n 117\t    const __m256 xc = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(x, _mm256_set1_ps(-3.0f)), v3);\n   118\t    const __m256 x2 = _mm256_mul_ps(xc, xc);\n   119\t    const __m256 num = _mm256_mul_ps(xc, 124\t}\n   125\t\n   126\tCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void distort_soft_avx2(const float* in_l, const float* in_r,\n   127\t                                                const float* threshold,\n   128\t                                                const float* dry_level, const float* wet_level,\n   129\t                                                float* out_l, float* out_r) noexcept {\n op_distort_tanh(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n   191\t    float* out_l = ctx.buffers->get(inst.out_buffer);\n   192\t    float* out_r = 310\t}\n   311\t\n   312\t// ============================================================================\n   313\t// DISTORT_FOLD: Wavefolder with ADAA (Antiderivative Antialiasing)\n   314\t// b < warmup; ++b) rig.block(b);\n   292\t    std::vector<double> ns(reps);\n   293\t    // One rep = REP_BLOCKS blocks spread evenly over the stimulus window, so\n   294\t    // every rep sees every stimulus segment (tanh cost is input-dependent;\n   295\t    // per-block timing would make p10/p90 measure the stimulus, not noise).\n   296\t    // Stimulus copies stay outside the timed region.\n   297\t    // An empty-program VM is timed interleaved with the real one and\n   298\t    // subtracted: process_block has ~450 ns of fixed per-block overhead,\n   299\t    // which otherwise swamps cheap opcodes like op_mul.\n   300\t    constexpr std::size_t REP_BLOCKS = 32;\n   301\t    Rig empty(*it, 0);\n   302\t    using clk = std::chrono::steady_clock;\n   303\t    auto dt = [](clk::time_point a, clk::time_point b) {\n   304\t        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::nano>(b - a).count();\n   305\t    };\n   306\t    for (std::size_t r = 0; r < reps; ++r) {\n   307\t        double t = 0.0;\n   308\t        for (std::size_t j = 0; j < REP_BLOCKS; ++j) {\n   309\t            rig.load(r + j * (STIM_BLOCKS / REP_BLOCKS));\n   310\t            auto t0 = clk::now();\n   311\t            empty.run();\n   312\t            auto t1 = clk::now();\n   313\t            rig.run();\n   314\t            auto t2 = clk::now();\n   315\t            t += dt(t1, t2) - dt(t0, t1);\n   316\t        }\n   317\t        ns[r] = t / (REP_BLOCKS * INSTANCES);\n   318\t    }\n   319\t    std::sort(ns.begin(), ns.end());\n   320\t    auto pct = [&](double p) { return ns[static_cast<std::size_t>(p * double(reps - 1))]; };\n   321\t    double med = pct(0.5), p10 = pct(0.1), p90 = pct(0.9);\n   322\t\n   323\t    std::string cpu = read_first_line(\"/proc/cpuinfo\", \"model name\");\n   324\t    std::string gov = read_first_line(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/cpufreq/scaling_governor\");\n   325\t    const char* isa = simd::name(simd::active());\n   326\t\n   327\t    if (json) {\n   328\t        std::printf(\"{\\\"opcode\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\\\"ns_per_block_median\\\":%.2f,\\\"ns_p10\\\":%.2f,\"\n   329\t                    \"\\\"ns_p90\\\":%.2f,\\\"reps\\\":%zu,\\\"isa\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\\\"cpu\\\":\\\"%s\\\",\"\n   330\t                    \"\\\"governor\\\":\\\"%s\\\"}\\n\",\n   331\t                    it->name, med, p10, p90, reps, isa, json_escape(cpu).c_str(),\n   332\t                    json_escape(gov).c_str());\n   333\t    } else {\n   334\t        std::printf(\"%-20s median %8.2f ns/block  p10 %8.2f  p90 %8.2f  (%s, %s)\\n\",\n   335\t                    it->name, med, p10, p90, isa, gov.c_str());\n   336\t    }\n   337\t    if (gov != \"performance\")\n   338\t        std::fprintf(stderr, \"warning: governor is '%s', not 'performance' \u2014 results unstable\\n\",\n   339\t                     gov.c_str());\n   340\t    return 0;\n   341\t}"
